  `EscrowCreate::preclaim` refuses a pseudo-account destination with tecNO_PERMISSION immediately after the tecNO_DST read, BEFORE the token helper and before the tag test (EscrowCreate.cpp:350-352). It is deliberately not amendment-gated, because every write to a discriminator field is.

  Two coupled rules behind an IoC cluster one bot repeated every ledger — 11 ledgers, 24 divergences, 77% of everything open. Neither rule fixes it alone.

- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a rested XRP residual rounds by mulRound, not by a plain ceiling** (`a8b01c7`, xrpl-validator)
  The bookend to `ff1e752`. There, `TOffer::limitOut` uses `ceilOutStrict` and gets a TRUE ceiling. Here, `flowCross`'s residual uses plain `mulRound`: afterCross.in = mulRound(afterCross.out, rate, takerAmount.in.asset(), true);
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): NFTokenBurn takes the token's OUTSTANDING OFFERS with it** (`1559d5f`, xrpl-validator)

- **fix(xrpl-ledger): AccountDelete walks the owner DIRECTORY — OwnerCount 0 is not "owns nothing"** (`a31296a`, xrpl-validator)
  Our only gate was `OwnerCount == 0`. #106322004 77C5E61D has OwnerCount 0 AND owns an Escrow, and those are not contradictory: an escrow that names this account as DESTINATION is linked into its owner directory, but the reserve sits with the escrow's CREATOR, so the destination's count never moves. The count says "owns nothing" while the directory says otherwise.

  rippled calls `flow(..., partialPayment = !tfFillOrKill, ...)`, so a FillOrKill offer either completes or moves nothing — and completion is the OUT side for a BUY (TakerPays delivered in full) but the IN side for a SELL. A sell bounded by the taker's own funding is complete when those funds are spent: `flowCross` clears BOTH sides of the remainder ("If offer crossing exhausted the account's funds don't create the …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): AMMWithdraw splits "no LP tokens" from "too few" — and the pool's own lines get hydrated** (`e982148`, xrpl-validator)
  `AMMWithdraw::preclaim` (AMMWithdraw.cpp:272-288) distinguishes two failures we collapsed into one: if (lpTokens <= beast::kZero) return tecAMM_BALANCE; // holding NONE if (*lpTokensWithdraw > lpTokens) return tecAMM_INVALID_TOKENS; // holding TOO FEW
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): DepositAuth — a payment needs the destination's permission** (`51300d1`, xrpl-validator)
  We had NO DepositAuth support at all: before this the only occurrence of the term in the crate was a comment about which flags an AMM account carries. So an account that had explicitly closed itself to unsolicited payments received them from us anyway.

  rippled's teardown erases the directory explicitly, before the account (AccountDelete.cpp): Keylet const ownerDirKeylet{keylet::ownerDir(accountID_)}; … if (view().exists(ownerDirKeylet) && !view().emptyDirDelete(ownerDirKeylet)) view().update(dst); view().erase(src);
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): ceilOut CLAMPS the input to the offer's own, and either side at zero consumes it** (`853e75a`, xrpl-validator)
  Two symptoms, one missing rule. `ceilOutImpl` ends with a step that is explicit in rippled's source, not a rounding artefact (Quality.cpp): Amounts result(MulRoundFunc(limit, quality.rate(), amount.in.asset(), roundUp), limit); // Clamp in if (result.in > amount.in) result.in = amount.in;
- **fix(harness): hydrate the NFT offer CREATOR's pages, not just the token's owner** (`fbb4f96`, xrpl-validator)
  `load_nft_pages_for_tx` took `Owner` OR `Account` for NFTokenCreateOffer. On a BUY offer `Owner` is the token's current holder — the seller — so the creator was skipped, and the creator is precisely the party who will RECEIVE the token.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): the AMM anchors only on a tip the taker could actually trade** (`1a96950`, xrpl-validator)
  `tryAMM` is handed `offers.tip().quality()`, and a tip beyond the taker's limit is not a tip the offer stream ever reaches — rippled then calls it with NO clob quality, which is `maxOffer`, and `limitOut` trims that to the limit instead. Our direct-walk AMM turn ran BEFORE the `q > threshold` break and anchored the slice to a level the taker had already rejected, sizing the fill off a price that was never available.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): NFTokenAcceptOffer weighs the buyer's funds — and the probe lets it see them** (`9c0d845`, xrpl-validator)
  9 of the 13 divergences in the 2026-08-14 fresh sweep were one cluster: `our_ter=tecOBJECT_NOT_FOUND` against `net_ter=tecINSUFFICIENT_FUNDS`, 1 mutation each side. TWO defects stacked, and fixing either alone measures WORSE than fixing neither.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): every payment strand runs ONE PASS — corpus byte parity 0 hits** (`950e63e`, xrpl-validator)
  The third and last part of the iteration model, and the SAME `if multi` gate the rounds carried in `4566c4e`. A pass ends when an offer is CONSUMED and `flow()` re-enters the strand; `single_pass` already implements that, but it was switched on only for multi-strand payments, so a lone strand walked the whole book in one call and MERGED what rippled does in separate iterations.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): rippleSend moves the sender's line ONCE, with the gross** (`bc756dd`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled's `rippleSend` (View.cpp) is one credit per party: saActual = saAmount + saTransitFee; rippleCredit(issuer, receiver, saAmount); // receiver gets the NET rippleCredit(sender, issuer, saActual); // sender pays the GROSS
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): limitOut takes the STRICT canonicaliser when the drops decide** (`ff1e752`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled's own comment on `TOffer::limitOut` (Offer.h:200): "It turns out that the ceil_out implementation has some slop in it, which ceil_out_strict removes." So an out-limited fill goes `ceilOutStrict` -> `mulRoundStrict` -> `canonicalizeRoundStrict`, not `mulRound`. After dividing down to offset -1 the two differ by one term (STAmount.cpp:1477 vs :1516): canonicalizeRound value += 9; canonicalizeRoundStrict value …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): the bridge's direct head prices a partial fill at the filed rate** (`7f8286b`, xrpl-validator)
  The rule `e2a9c99` gave the two bridge legs, never given to the third fill site in the same function. An offer carries two rates: the one it is FILED under (low 64 bits of `BookDirectory`, a 16-digit mantissa fixed at creation) and its current TakerPays/TakerGets, which drifts every time a residual is re-rounded. rippled prices a PARTIAL fill at the filed rate and a FULL one at the amounts.
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): one AMM consumption per payment-engine iteration** (`4566c4e`, xrpl-validator)
  "At any payment engine iteration, AMM offer can only be consumed once" (BookStep.cpp:818). When a payment wants more pool liquidity than one AMM offer gives, rippled ENDS the pass and `flow()` re-enters the strand for the next one — and every iteration writes the maker's residual, so N iterations are N roundings. We took Fibonacci slices inside a single pass and rounded once.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): ask the walk what a strand spent, don't difference the balance** (`8d8d2e6`, xrpl-validator)
  Third instance of the defect `44c20d9` fixed for a hop's carry and `45a7092` for the reverse pass — and exactly the `signed_add(before, !after)` shape 45a7092's note said to go looking for. `strand_pass` reported its input consumption by differencing the sender's spend-leg balance, and a balance holds 16 significant digits TOTAL, so whatever the sender already holds of the spend currency eats the low end of the …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): the precise measurement must account for the fee the walk debits** (`1f31546`, xrpl-validator)
  A defect in `45a7092`. Its precise path is `granted - rem_in`, but `rem_in` tracks only the NET the walk moved — the input transfer fee is debited SEPARATELY by `line_adjust`. So on a fee-bearing hop `precise` is the net while the differenced balance `d` is the gross, the two disagree by the WHOLE FEE (1e-3 for a 1.001 issuer, a thousand times the 1e-9 agreement threshold), and the guard rejected the precise value …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): measure the REVERSE pass's consumption precisely too** (`45a7092`, xrpl-validator)
  `44c20d9` fixed the forward carry and I did not notice the same defect one level up. `measure_hop` worked out how much a trial consumed by differencing a GRANTED balance — 16 significant digits TOTAL, so the grant itself eats the low end of the answer — while `cross_engine_to` was already RETURNING the precise remaining input and the code threw it away with `let (rw, _, _) = ...`.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): reprice a rested remainder in BOTH directions** (`afb133f`, xrpl-validator)
  The one-way clamp on the reprice — take `derived` only when it moves the residual DOWN — was a workaround for #105930662 40FB322EC16C from when the reprice still used the RAW ratio. `070ca31` fixed the rate itself and that ledger is byte-exact now, so the guard had outlived its cause and did nothing except suppress the reprice wherever it needed to move the residual UP.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): measure a hop's carry at full precision, not by differencing** (`44c20d9`, xrpl-validator)
  The forward pass measured what a hop produced by differencing the sender's BALANCE. A balance holds 16 significant digits TOTAL, so whatever the sender already holds of the intermediate eats the low end of the addend — the hop is fine, the measurement is not.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): an INPUT-limited fill rounds the offer's output DOWN** (`ca17418`, xrpl-validator)
  BookStep.cpp:653, verbatim, with rippled's own rationale above it: ofrAmt = offer.limitIn(ofrAmt, inLmt, /* roundUp */ false);
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): stamount_signed_add ROUNDS its operands, not truncates** (`6798100`, xrpl-validator)
  An STAmount operand is already canonical in rippled — it was rounded to 16 digits when it was produced — so reducing an over-precise operand by truncation drops exactly the digit that decides the result. `norm16` truncates.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): reprice a rested remainder at the ENCODED ratio** (`070ca31`, xrpl-validator)
  Two changes to `offer.rs`; only the second moves any ledger.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): charge the input transfer fee once — the walk owns it** (`41ef4ac`, xrpl-validator)
  `payment.rs` and `offer.rs` both quoted BookStep.cpp:770 and both applied the issuer's rate, and neither comment knew the other existed. `measure_hop` GRANTS a balance and differences it, and the walk debits the fee per fill, so the fee was already inside `consumed` before the reverse pass grossed it up again — the rate compounded.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): price bridged fills at the filed BookDirectory rate** (`e2a9c99`, xrpl-validator)
  An offer carries two rates and they are not the same number: the rate it is FILED under (low 64 bits of `BookDirectory`, 16-digit mantissa, fixed at creation) and its current `TakerPays / TakerGets`, which drifts every time a residual is re-rounded. rippled prices a PARTIAL fill at the filed rate and a FULL one at the offer's own amounts. The direct walk has done this since `full_offer`; `cross_bridged` priced both …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): store offer residuals at STAmount precision, not exactly** (`4556384`, xrpl-validator)

Being more precise than rippled is a defect: the validator serialises the stored …

- **feat(xrpl-node): DX_VALCHECK compares EXACTLY, at STAmount precision** (`adca53a`, xrpl-validator)
  The target is byte parity: a validator serialises the stored decimal into the SLE and hashes it, so one digit apart is a different state hash and a diverged node however small the number. The old check could not certify that — it parsed both sides into f64 and compared against a 1e-12 tolerance.

  CLOSES THE AMM VALUE FAMILY: 23 hits at gate 46 -> 0. #105813899 2C8C37F9 was the last one, 55/55 with no DX_VALCHECK output at all.

- **fix(xrpl-ledger): mint real LP tokens for AMMCreate and single-asset deposits** (`85bb7df`, xrpl-validator)
  Both mint sites were hardcoded 1e7 placeholders, documented as "the magnitude is oracle-corrected downstream". They were 23 of the 83 value divergences at gate 46 and by far the largest in the backlog — relative errors up to 3e5, where the whole remaining OfferCreate tail sits below 2e-4.
- **fix(xrpl-node): choose the hydration RPC per LEDGER, not per sweep script** (`25c9e31`, xrpl-validator)
  The gate corpora AGE OUT of our LAN validator's rolling window (~290k ledgers on 2026-08-10, 105919185-106209950), so a fixture that hydrated locally last month must come from s2 today. A server pinned per script cannot express that, and both failure directions were live: * `batch2sweep.sh` pins `--rpc <lan>` while 7 of its 26 ledgers had rolled out of the window (105909285 105909624 105910649 105911834 105912291 …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): match rippled's AMM slice sizing on direct offer crossing** (`b2c47da`, xrpl-validator)
  Two coupled rules, both read off libxrpl's own trace of #106143011 4607B92B against the RLUSD/XRP pool and confirmed in rippled's source.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a payment pays the INPUT issuer's TransferRate** (`c99ebb2`, xrpl-validator)
  `apply_path_payment` charged the OUTPUT issuer's rate (`want_rate`) and let the input through free. A payment sets `ownerPaysTransferFee_ = false`, so `trIn = transferRate(book_.in.account)`: the SENDER parts with `amount × rate` and the counterparty receives `amount` (BookStep.cpp:737-739). `apply_iou_direct` has always done this for the same-currency case — the crossing path never did.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): reprice bridged leg A after the mid-leg rounds up** (`07f2e32`, xrpl-validator)
  9bb5ceb rounded the bridged XRP mid-leg UP to whole drops and left `gets_in` priced from the FRACTIONAL value it had before. So leg A took a whole drop and paid for a fraction less of one, every time the rounding did anything.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): the bridged XRP mid-leg must CEIL to whole drops, not truncate** (`9bb5ceb`, xrpl-validator)
  Autobridged crossing routes value through XRP, and that mid-leg has to land on whole drops. `cross_bridged` truncated it — `me_rescale(xrp, 0, false)` — so one leg moved a drop more than the other could absorb and the pass came out skewed in both directions at once.
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): DX_CLAMP — and it retires the ceil_in flip for good** (`9e475fe`, xrpl-validator)
  The 2026-08-08 attempt to flip the taker-clamped XRP floor to a ceil regressed #105284280 (78/78 -> 77/78) and took #105091578 from 0 value hits to 21. The note left behind said the next attempt had to establish, first, whether the twelve one-drop-short residuals reach that branch at all.
- **fix(xrpl-node): prefetch NFT pages for NFTokenModify — it is tt 61, not 25..=29** (`5aad67e`, xrpl-validator)
  `is_nft_tx_type` was `(25..=29).contains(&tt)` — Mint, Burn, CreateOffer, CancelOffer, AcceptOffer. NFTokenModify (XLS-46) is tt 61: it POSTDATES that contiguous range, so a transaction whose entire job is to locate a token and rewrite its URI never had the owner's NFTokenPages fetched. libxrpl's `nft::findToken` then searched a page set that was never loaded and returned tecNO_ENTRY on a token that plainly exists.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): token escrow — Cancel and Finish never released the token** (`6ef30c4`, xrpl-validator)
  #106179351: 413 txs, 221 matched. `EscrowCancel attempted=192 MATCH=0 DIVERGE-MUT=192` -- every EscrowCancel in the ledger, all the same shape, ours 3 mutations against mainnet's 8. FFI PROBE: CLEAN, so ours.
- **feat(xrpl-node): FFI_VALCHECK — the oracle rule now works at VALUE level** (`c7d4625`, xrpl-validator)
  `PROBE: CLEAN` has only ever meant libxrpl touched the same OBJECTS mainnet did. It never said libxrpl wrote the same NUMBERS, because the entire parity verdict is a mutation-KEY comparison. So when 9bc95c8 made value divergences visible on the native side, there was no way to attribute them: 175 value hits and no verdict on whether any of them were ours.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): rank payment strands by UPPER BOUND, not by a realised trial** (`0f55ba5`, xrpl-validator)
  `payment.rs`'s round loop trialled every strand, measured what each pass actually delivered, and picked the best spent-per-delivered. That is the model this repo already REFUTED for offer crossing in 03c2cb9 -- `ActiveStrands::activateNext` sorts by `qualityUpperBound` best-first, DROPS any strand whose bound misses limitQuality, and the loop takes the first candidate whose pass is not rejected, `continue`-ing past …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): one strand per named path, and stop gating hops with the payment's limit** (`868cf89`, xrpl-validator)
  #106156904 341030105165 -- ours tecPATH_DRY with 1 mutation vs mainnet tesSUCCESS with 7. Circular tfPartialPayment|tfLimitQuality, SendMax 7.203545999999999 USDT for 0.095 SOL, and `Paths` carries SIX paths.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): NFTokenCreateOffer never charged for the object it creates** (`a56ccbd`, xrpl-validator)
  #106154459 A55AD53EEFA2 -- ours tesSUCCESS with 5 mutations vs mainnet tecINSUFFICIENT_RESERVE with 1. All five EXTRA: the NFTokenOffer plus both directory pages.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a rested residual keeps its ORIGINAL quality in its AMOUNTS** (`850da82`, xrpl-validator)
  The book page for a partially crossed offer has always been built from the offer as REQUESTED (`tp0`/`tg0`) -- rippled keeps a partial fill at its original price. The AMOUNTS written into that offer did not follow: they were `TakerGets - consumed`, i.e. reduced by the currency actually handed over.
- **feat(xrpl-node): DX_VALCHECK — compare the VALUES we wrote, not just the keys** (`9bc95c8`, xrpl-validator)
  The differential probe's verdict is a mutation-set comparison, and a mutation set is KEYS. Every value a transaction writes is invisible to it.

Circular tfPartialPayment|tfNoRippleDirect, SendMax 0.02117670765212899 mXRP for 2.302493172774841 CORE over a FOUR-leg chain of pools, mXRP -> BitX -> BTC -> CORE. One strand, so 89bed1e is inert here: same symptom as #106143011, different regime.

So this time I grepped every transactor that emits the code -- rippled has tecDST_TAG_NEEDED in SEVEN, we had it in TWO.

  Completes the AMM bridge slice-sizing family. Every divergence from the 2026-08-07 fresh batch now matches mainnet.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): single-path AMM takes maxOffer, clamped through the pool BOTH ways** (`3180e04`, xrpl-validator)

  DX_SEL was added in dbb9110 to find a ledger where our estimate-based strand selection differed from rippled's upper-bound one. It found the answer, the answer was acted on, and the detector outlived its premise: since 03c2cb9 the engine SELECTS by that upper-bound model (`order`), so DX_SEL was comparing an abandoned model against the live one and reporting "mismatches" that described nothing real.

  Four changes to the bridged crossing, all read off libxrpl's trace on #105912454 FE592890B233 (24 mutations against mainnet's 17).
- **fix(ledger): one AMM turn per pass, and a rejected pass ends the crossing** (`8152aca`, xrpl-validator)
  Three pieces, each only visible once the previous one landed, all read off libxrpl's own trace rather than inferred.
- **fix(ledger): judge a fill's achieved quality exactly, as rippled does** (`133fd89`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled discards a pass outright when the quality it actually realised misses the limit: if (limitQuality && q < *limitQuality && (!adjustedRemOut || !withinRelativeDistance(q, *limitQuality, Number(1, -7))))
- **fix(ledger): round a fill's XRP input by which side bounds it** (`d6f7589`, xrpl-validator)
  The taker's input was always ceiled to whole drops. rippled's direction depends on which side of the fill is fixed — its rev/fwd split: - the taker's remaining WANT bounds it: the input is computed FROM that output (`rev`) and rounds UP, so the taker pays enough; - the maker's FUNDING bounds it: the output is what is fixed and the input follows it (`fwd`), rounding DOWN.
- **feat(ffi): capture libxrpl's decision trace under XRPL_FFI_TRACE** (`8b5a197`, xrpl-validator)
  The shim already had a CapturingSink wired into xrpl_apply_with_mutations and surfaced through xrpl_result_last_fatal — but gated at Severity::Fatal, so everything rippled narrates was thrown away. Lift it to Trace when XRPL_FFI_TRACE is set, and dump it per transaction with XRPL_FFI_TRACE_TX=<hash prefix>. Both OFF by default: gate runs stay byte-identical and trace capture is neither free nor small.
- **feat(ledger): reverse pass — size each payment hop to what the next needs** (`9ac6bcc`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled flows a strand in TWO passes: a REVERSE pass walking back from the requested output working out how much input each step needs (StrandFlow.h:105-230, `for (auto i = s; i--;) strand[i]->rev(...)`, re-executing a limiting step when one binds), then a FORWARD pass bounded by what is actually available. Ours was forward-only, so an intermediate hop sold its WHOLE carry rather than buying what the hop after it …
- **fix(ledger): the POOL can activate a strand, and an active strand reaps** (`6e631e7`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled filters strands on `qualityUpperBound` — an OPTIMISTIC bound on what the strand could conceivably do (StrandFlow.h:696-699, `qualityUpperBound(sb, *strand) < *limitQuality => continue`). For a book with an AMM that bound includes the pool. A strand whose every BOOK level is worse than the limit therefore still gets built, its offer stream still steps, and the dead offers it steps past are still reaped — even …
- **feat(ledger): flow the default path alongside Paths, per pass** (`99f8a7a`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled ALWAYS builds the default path beside the ones named in Paths (unless tfNoRippleDirect), flows each strand, and applies the best-quality PASS each round before re-evaluating (StrandFlow.h:682-805). We ran the specified chain alone.
- **refactor(ledger): one strand code path in Payment** (`c858f1f`, xrpl-validator)
  The payment had two execution shapes — a hop chain when Paths named usable intermediate currencies, and a separate direct cross_engine_to otherwise. They were the same walk: with no hops the chain is [spend, want], the loop runs exactly one hop with last=true, benef=dest and want_cap=want_gross, and issues the identical cross_engine_to call the direct branch made. The inflight snapshot iterates `hops` and so is …
- **feat(ledger): AMMDeposit tfTwoAsset equalDepositLimit + tecAMM_FAILED** (`a533219`, xrpl-validator)
  Amount and Amount2 on a two-asset deposit are MAXIMA, not the amounts deposited. rippled derives one side from the other at the pool's ratio and fails the transaction outright when NEITHER derivation fits inside what was offered (AMMDeposit.cpp:721-787): frac = amount / amountBalance (Number, 16 digits) tokensAdj = adjustLPTokens(lpt, multiply(lpt, frac, DOWNWARD)) frac = tokensAdj / lpt (adjustFracByTokens) amt2Dep …
- **fix(ledger): measure a payment hop's carry, don't subtract a sentinel** (`7efe2fd`, xrpl-validator)
  An intermediate hop of a multi-hop payment has no real ceiling, so it was handed a sentinel want-cap of (9.99e15, 60) and its output recovered as `want_cap - remaining`. That cannot work. `me_rescale` saturates (saturating_mul), so rescaling the sentinel down to a fill's exponent pins it at u128::MAX — and it does so again on the NEXT fill, resetting the running remainder and ERASING every fill before it. The …
- **fix(ledger): remove self-crossed offers off the book tip** (`b733ae7`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled DELETES the taker's own offers off the tip rather than crossing them — BookStep.cpp:415-455 `limitSelfCrossQuality`, which explains the choice itself: "We could skip over the self offer in the book and only cross offers that are not our own. This would make a lot of sense, but we don't do it ... we can only operate on the tip of the order book. We can't leave an offer behind -- it would sit on the tip and …
- **fix(ledger): CredentialCreate joins both owner directories** (`be244f0`, xrpl-validator)
  A credential is inserted into the issuer's AND the subject's owner directory, but is owned only by the issuer — CredentialCreate.cpp:147-174: "Added to both dirs, owned only by issuer. CredentialAccept will transfer ownership to subject." So the issuer's dirInsert carries the single adjustOwnerCount(+1) and the subject's adds no reserve at all. A self-issued credential (subject == account) skips the subject insert …
- **fix(ledger): apply tfSetfAuth to the trust line** (`4bac28e`, xrpl-validator)
  We honoured tfSetfAuth in preclaim's redundancy test but never APPLIED it. `do_apply` handled tfSetNoRipple / tfClearNoRipple / tfSetFreeze / tfClearFreeze and had no branch for the auth bit, so a TrustSet whose only effect is authorisation left the line untouched entirely.
- **fix(ledger): align IOU addition the way STAmount does** (`d5a08c8`, xrpl-validator)
  `STAmount operator+` brings both operands to the LARGER exponent by integer division: while (ov1 < ov2) { vv1 /= 10; ++ov1; } while (ov2 < ov1) { vv2 /= 10; ++ov2; }
- **fix(probe): hydrate trust lines for the currencies named in Paths** (`549abb1`, xrpl-validator)
  We hydrated only the Amount and SendMax currencies, never the intermediate hops. A payment that ripples through a third currency therefore saw NO trust line for that hop, ran trustCreate for a line that already exists, and invented a directory page to hold it.
- **fix(ledger): split a full NFToken page before inserting** (`9a82325`, xrpl-validator)
  `page_insert` had NO capacity check: it located the page and inserted, so a full page silently grew to 33 tokens — past kDirMaxTokensPerPage. rippled splits first (NFTokenHelpers.cpp getPageForToken): the lower part moves to a NEW page keyed below, the rest stays, and the predecessor is relinked. That Created + Modified pair is exactly what mainnet emits.
- **fix(ledger): consolidate NFToken pages when a removal shrinks one** (`761df02`, xrpl-validator)
  `page_remove` handled an EMPTIED page (delete, relink neighbours) but did nothing when a page merely shrank. rippled consolidates on every removal (NFTokenHelpers.cpp removeToken): "The current page isn't empty. Update it and then try to consolidate pages. Note that this consolidation attempt may actually merge three pages into one!"
- **fix(ledger): tfOneAssetWithdrawAll empties the LP position** (`e12436b`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled's isWithdrawAll covers BOTH tfWithdrawAll (0x00020000) AND tfOneAssetWithdrawAll (0x00040000) — AMMWithdraw.cpp:1133-1138: if ((tx[sfFlags] & (tfWithdrawAll | tfOneAssetWithdrawAll)) != 0u) return WithdrawAll::Yes;
- **fix(ledger): remove the taker's own offer even when nothing crosses** (`70ea97e`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled deletes the taker's OWN offer off the tip of the book during offer crossing, and its commentary spells out why (BookStep.cpp:415-455): "We could skip over the self offer in the book and only cross offers that are not our own. This would make a lot of sense, but we don't do it. Part of the rationale is that we can only operate on the tip of the order book. We can't leave an offer behind -- it would sit on the …
- **fix(ledger): enforce lsfRequireAuth on the receiving trust line** (`a67eeca`, xrpl-validator)
  We never modelled lsfRequireAuth in the liquidity path at all — only in trust_set.rs for line-deletion decisions. rippled enforces it at STRAND CONSTRUCTION (DirectStep.cpp:430-437, DirectIPaymentStep::check): auto const authField = (src_ > dst_) ? lsfHighAuth : lsfLowAuth; if (sleSrc->isFlag(lsfRequireAuth) && !sleLine->isFlag(authField) && (*sleLine)[sfBalance] == beast::kZero) return terNO_AUTH;

  Our preflight required BOTH Subject and Issuer. rippled requires at least ONE (CredentialDelete.cpp preflight: `if (!subject && !issuer) return temMALFORMED`) and resolves the pair in preclaim with
- **fix(ledger): funding outranks expiry in OfferCreate preclaim** (`5582a93`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled's OfferCreate::preclaim (:171) runs
- **fix(probe): hydrate the ISSUERS' owner directories for OfferCreate** (`e10fe45`, xrpl-validator)
  A crossing OfferCreate can hand the taker an IOU it has never held, creating a trust line that appends to BOTH sides' owner directories. The probe hydrated only the ACCOUNT's, never the issuers' — so for a real issuer, whose directory runs to thousands of pages, dir_insert could not see the existing directory and invented a fresh root where mainnet modified a tail page.
- **fix(ledger): a bridge leg with a pool but no book carries nothing** (`413368a`, xrpl-validator)
  All 8 cases were Flags=65536 exactly — tfPassive, buy side, IOU<->IOU with no XRP leg — so every one takes the auto-bridge path. That uniformity is what made the cluster tractable.
- **fix(ledger): pass the page hint when a payment tears down a trust line** (`95eb59c`, xrpl-validator)
  A line spent to zero mid-payment reverts to default and is deleted, which must remove it from BOTH owner directories. We called owner_dir_remove for both sides but passed None as the page hint, while TrustSet's trustDelete passes the real LowNode/HighNode (trust_set.rs:371-375).
- **fix(ledger): AMMWithdraw by LPTokenIn pays out both pool assets** (`e7b7d4d`, xrpl-validator)
  tfLPToken (0x00010000) withdrawals name only how many LPTokens to redeem and receive BOTH pool assets in proportion — the transaction carries no Amount or Amount2 at all. Our apply path only ever moved Amount/Amount2, found neither, and so burned the LP tokens while paying out nothing. That is exactly why every case was missing the two RippleStates and the AccountRoot a payout touches.
- **feat(ledger): NFTokenMint carrying Amount also rests a sell offer** (`2488343`, xrpl-validator)
  featureNFTokenMintOffer: a mint with `Amount` mints the token AND rests a sell offer in one transaction. rippled factors this as `nft::tokenOfferCreateApply` and calls it from both NFTokenCreateOffer and NFTokenMint (NFTokenMint.cpp:312-330). We minted but never created the offer, losing exactly three mutations every time — the NFTokenOffer, its sell-offer directory page, and the owner directory entry. 11 cases in …
- **fix(ledger): OfferCreate reserve is pre-fee, and expiry precedes it** (`d753d7a`, xrpl-validator)
  Two mainnet singles, both from rMWVf1qJsgHgEd1Tuy378Zs53noKh4BujK.
- **fix(differential_probe): put the RPC server in the cache key** (`52a567d`, xrpl-validator)
  The dxcache is one directory shared by every probe run on the box, and they do not all talk to the same node. The nightly scout hydrates from our own the reference node (`runner.sh diff <seq> http://[host]`); re-probing an older find has to use s2, because the reference node's ~17k-ledger window rolled past it long ago. The key was method+params only, so one server's answers were served for the other server's question.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): an AMM deposit must fund its XRP side after reserve** (`11a9c6c`, xrpl-validator)
  #105893158 85C32164 (7v1). We had only the reserve guard from 385e129, which asks whether the depositor clears its reserve at all. rippled additionally checks that the XRP side is FUNDABLE, and measures that against the reserve the depositor will owe AFTERWARDS: `xrpLiquid` is taken with the owner count bumped by one when there is no LPToken line yet, because the deposit is about to open one (AMMDeposit.cpp:230-244, …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): token escrow, and give escrows their directory entries** (`5704d68`, xrpl-validator)
  #105823810 6AB38288 escrows 3750000 STSH. We rejected it temBAD_AMOUNT and applied nothing (0v6); mainnet built it in 7 nodes. This is the escrow lead the handoff has carried for weeks -- it finally has a fixture.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): cap a direct IOU payment at what the destination can receive** (`cce3c8d`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled caps a payment's final issuer->dest step at `creditLimit(dst,issuer) - heldByDst`, floored at zero (DirectStepI::maxPaymentFlow, DirectStep.cpp:487), so a destination already at or over its own trust limit receives nothing and the strand is dry. `apply_path_payment` has carried that cap since 04a1586; that commit called the direct route the same rule "in principle" and left it alone for want of a failing …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): finish CheckCreate's destination checks** (`edf4462`, xrpl-validator)
  Our CheckCreate preclaim stopped at "the destination exists". rippled's goes on to ask whether that destination will actually accept the check, and #105846674 F6CC9A594B17 is the case we were missing: a VRTY airdrop check to rnabZzjg, whose flags 0x120000 carry lsfRequireDestTag, sent with no DestinationTag. Mainnet claims the fee in one mutation; we created the Check and both directory entries in seven.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): honour the NFT offer opt-out on Owner and Destination** (`8b23677`, xrpl-validator)
  An account can opt out of receiving NFT offers with `lsfDisallowIncomingNFTokenOffer` (0x04000000), and rippled's shared `tokenOfferCreatePreclaim` (NFTokenHelpers.cpp:824) honours that for BOTH accounts an offer names: a `Destination` must exist (tecNO_DST), and neither it nor the token's `Owner` may carry the flag -- either is tecNO_PERMISSION. We checked neither.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a frozen holder can fund nothing** (`1c46cda`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled reads spendable IOU through `accountHolds` with `fhZERO_IF_FROZEN`, so a holder whose line the issuer has frozen has NO funds at all, however large the balance. `isFrozen` (RippleStateHelpers.cpp:127) is the issuer's GLOBAL freeze, or the ISSUER's side of the line -- `(issuer > account) ? lsfHighFreeze : lsfLowFreeze`. Our `available()` read the balance and nothing else, so a frozen holder could trade freely.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a bridged pass needs its book composition within the limit** (`625399c`, xrpl-validator)
  #105807256 84FD7DC8, the last divergence in the harness. A tfSell|tfPassive OfferCreate selling 3105.662739 BBRL for 607.478737 RLUSD: mainnet rests it in 4 nodes, we crossed 8 objects (12v4, all extra, nothing missing).
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): tick re-derivation divides with the STAmount rule** (`1edaa63`, xrpl-validator)
  #105777146 9BA91A9E sells 0.00059094 WETH for 1 XRP. The WETH issuer publishes TickSize 6, so the rate rounds up to 6 significant digits and the non-exact side is re-derived before placing. We derived TakerGets 0.000590939712330548 where mainnet stored 0.0005909397123305481 -- one ulp higher -- and that single ulp moved the rested offer to book level ...ABFB5801 where mainnet wrote ...ABFB5800. 4v4, the ledger's …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a crossing that exhausts the account rests no remainder** (`bb522bf`, xrpl-validator)
  "If offer crossing exhausted the account's funds don't create the offer" (OfferCreate.cpp:479-484). This is the companion to the pre-crossing `accountFunds` clamp from 1de6d87: that one bounds what may be SPENT, this one bounds what may REST.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): carry the trailing reap across book levels** (`ba73d4f`, xrpl-validator)
  The trailing step added in 130c246 stopped at the end of the page chain of the level it had been crossing on. rippled's stream spans the whole BOOK: `OfferStream::step` carries no quality test at all, so after a consumed offer it keeps advancing — and reaping — across quality levels until it reaches a LIVE offer, and only then does `execOffer`'s `checkQualityThreshold` return false and end the walk …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): step past a satisfied fill, and reap dust-backed offers** (`130c246`, xrpl-validator)
  Two more of `OfferStream::step`'s removal semantics, both needed to close #105778999 6B2A11B3 (5v15, missing 10, extra 0) -- a circular self-pay, SendMax 140 XRP into POSAA, tfPartialPayment.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): reap a book level's dead offers before consulting the pool** (`01f09ba`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled steps the offer stream BEFORE it asks the AMM, and every dead offer stepped past is removed whether or not anything crossed: if (offers.step()) // BookStep.cpp:835 if (tryAMM(offers.tip().quality())) // anchor = first LIVE offer do { ... } while (offers.step()); else tryAMM(std::nullopt); // exhausted -> maxOffer return {offers.permToRemove(), ...}; // BookStep.cpp:852
- **fix(clawback): decode the base58 holder from Amount.issuer** (`f6ed033`, xrpl-validator)
  Clawback resolves the token holder from the nested Amount.issuer field, but misc.rs::decode_account_id was hex-only. The probe hex-normalises only top-level ACCOUNT_FIELDS, never a nested amount object, so Amount.issuer arrives as a base58 r-address -- decoding it hex-only returned None and the transactor rejected a valid clawback as Malformed. A spurious reject is consensus-relevant: we would drop a tx mainnet …
- **fix(nftoken): decode the base58 Issuer field on authorised-minter mints** (`881e160`, xrpl-validator)
  NFTokenMint's decode_account_id was hex-only, but the probe does not hex-normalise the Issuer field (it is absent from ACCOUNT_FIELDS), so an authorised-minter mint's base58 Issuer silently failed to decode and fell back to tx.account -- the minter. One wrong account cascaded two ways: MintedNFTokens was credited to the minter instead of the issuer, and the NFTokenID embedded the minter, so the token's low-96 -- …
- **fix(differential_probe): hydrate the issuer's owner-dir tail for CheckCash** (`7eb0dca`, xrpl-validator)
  CheckCash creates the delivered IOU's trust line, which the ISSUER co-owns -- but native_read_keys loaded only the casher's owner-dir root, so an issuer with a multi-page directory was never hydrated and dir_insert invented a fresh root instead of appending to the existing last page.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): implement AMMWithdraw tfWithdrawAll teardown** (`a7bd6a0`, xrpl-validator)
  tfWithdrawAll fell through the partial-withdraw path: it withdrew NO assets (no Amount fields to loop over) and burned a fixed default LPToken amount, leaving the LPToken trust line Modified-at-zero instead of torn down.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): honour tfPassive -- don't consume equal-quality makers** (`77f6f39`, xrpl-validator)
  We never read tfPassive: a passive offer was crossed like a normal one. A passive offer crosses only STRICTLY better makers and rests behind equal-quality ones (rippled OfferCreate.cpp:396 `++threshold`). Our rate encoding is inverted (lower = better, monotone in the u64), so tighten the threshold by one ULP for passive offers; the existing q<=threshold and maker-rate gates then exclude the equal level.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): re-check a maker's actual quality, not just its book page** (`7e592e9`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled compares a crossing maker's ACTUAL getRate to the taker's limit, not merely the quantized book-directory level it rests in. An offer can sit in a page that ties the threshold while its exact rate is one ULP worse, and rippled leaves such an offer untouched.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): cross an underfunded maker at book quality, don't rest** (`84898d4`, xrpl-validator)
  The section-1 achieved-quality break (offer.rs) was calibrated for the #105780948 IoC tecKILLED, where the TAKER's own side bounds the fill and the floored output realises worse than the limit. It also fired, wrongly, on a plain offer crossing a maker that is merely UNDERFUNDED at a book quality equal to the taker's limit: the <=1e-7 round-up on `pay` for the small maker-funds-clamped fill tips the achieved rate …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): cap payment delivery at the destination's trust limit** (`04a1586`, xrpl-validator)
  The last TER-class divergence cluster (6: 105740164/105741244/105741859, 2 each) was not the two-hop AMM strand bug the handoff suspected. rippled's final delivery step DirectStepI::maxPaymentFlow (DirectStep.cpp:487) limits what a payment can land on the destination to creditLimit(dst,issuer) minus what it already holds, so a destination at or over its own trust limit receives nothing and the whole flow is …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): honour keepRoot when a directory page empties** (`34fca11`, xrpl-validator)
  dir_remove always deleted an emptied root page -- rippled's keepRoot=false behaviour -- with no way to ask for anything else. Most owner-directory removals pass true, so we emitted the page as Deleted (:2) where mainnet emits it Modified (:1).
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): CheckCancel unlinks the Check from both owner directories** (`b79f40f`, xrpl-validator)
  A Check is linked into TWO owner directories -- the writer's via sfOwnerNode and the destination's via sfDestinationNode. CheckCreate inserts both (check.rs) and CheckCash removes both, but CheckCancel removed neither: it deleted the object and adjusted the reserve, leaving two dangling directory entries.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): OfferCreate crossing spends only accountFunds, not TakerGets** (`1de6d87`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled clamps the crossing input to the taker's actual funds -- "Don't send more than our balance", OfferCreate.cpp:399-401, where inStartBalance = accountFunds(psb, accountID_, takerAmount.in, ...) is the XRP balance minus reserve. TakerGets bounds what may REST, never what may be spent. We clamped only at TakerGets, so an underfunded offer crossed with money the account did not have.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a Payment to a pseudo-account is tecNO_PERMISSION** (`3a718aa`, xrpl-validator)
  An AMM, Vault, or LoanBroker account receives value only through its own transaction type — never an ordinary Payment. rippled rejects it with tecNO_PERMISSION (Payment.cpp:635, isPseudoAccount(sleDst)), where a pseudo-account is any AccountRoot carrying a designator field: sfAMMID, sfVaultID, or sfLoanBrokerID. The engine had no such check and delivered.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): CheckCash requires exactly one of Amount / DeliverMin** (`609c827`, xrpl-validator)
  Preflight required Amount, so it rejected every DeliverMin cash-out as temMALFORMED — a valid transaction the network accepts, which is a consensus-relevant divergence (we'd drop a tx mainnet applies). rippled's rule is an XOR: bool(Amount) == bool(DeliverMin) => temMALFORMED (CheckCash.cpp:57). Amount is a fixed cash-out; DeliverMin a floor for a partial one.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): reject offers by achieved quality and skip expired ones; keep the cleanup on tecKILLED** (`a823d98`, xrpl-validator)
  Three faults in book crossing, uncovered together on two IoC offers that mainnet leaves completely untouched.
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): DX_BOOK trace for the book walk's quality gate** (`eb19a38`, xrpl-validator)
  Prints each directory's encoded quality against the taker's threshold at the point the walk decides to break, in the same style as DX_AMM and DX_BRIDGE. The crossing gate is a 64-bit encoded-rate comparison where a single ulp decides whether an offer is reachable, and there was no way to see either side of it without a rebuild.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): tecOBJECT_NOT_FOUND for missing NFT offers, tecAMM_BALANCE for over-withdraw** (`ce27e48`, xrpl-validator)
  Two unrelated result-code gaps, both fee-only on mainnet.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): correct the credential keylet, allow self-issuance, hydrate it** (`08515b3`, xrpl-validator)
  Four faults stacked on one transaction, each hiding the next.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): merging past an oracle's ten-entry cap is tecARRAY_TOO_LARGE** (`bfc83f9`, xrpl-validator)
  OracleSet is an upsert. Overflowing kMaxOracleDataSeries by MERGING the transaction's series into the existing object is a tec, not a tem: the transaction is well formed and only the resulting object is too big, so the fee is claimed (OracleSet.cpp:168). temARRAY_TOO_LARGE is a separate preflight check on the transaction's own array (line 46), which is not what these hit.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): AccountDelete fee is one owner reserve, read from the ledger** (`3cebd6a`, xrpl-validator)
  AccountDelete::calculateBaseFee returns calculateOwnerReserveFee(view, tx) — "the fee required for AccountDelete is one owner reserve". That comes from the ledger's fee settings and is not a constant: the 2024 vote cut the increment from 2 XRP to 0.2, so a hardcoded 2_000_000 rejects every present-day AccountDelete.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): charge the issuer's TransferRate on payment delivery** (`2b1beae`, xrpl-validator)
  The engine ignored TransferRate entirely, so every payment that moved an IOU between two non-issuers delivered the gross amount. rippled charges the issuer's rate on the strand's last step: DirectStepI issues to the destination and qualitiesSrcIssues sets srcQOut = transferRate(issuer) whenever the previous step redeems (DirectStep.cpp:765), which for a payment is always — BookStep reports DebtDirection::Redeems …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): a payment cannot open the destination's trust line** (`e27d088`, xrpl-validator)
  Every payment strand ends by rippling the delivered IOU from its issuer to the destination, and that final step is a DirectStepI whose check opens with "Since this is a payment a trust line must be present" — no line yields terNO_LINE (DirectStep.cpp:423). Because every strand shares that same last step, one missing line invalidates all of them, and a flow with no surviving strands is tecPATH_DRY.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): no AMM liquidity in permissioned-domain books — corpus 100%** (`84ced29`, xrpl-validator)
  BookStep::tryAMM returns early when book_.domain is set ("amm doesn't support domain yet", BookStep.cpp:820), so a pool never participates in a domain-scoped book. cross_engine_to discovered the AMM regardless of domain, letting a domain offer cross a pool that rippled does not admit.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): payments never autobridge — AMM over-mutation via a synthesized XRP leg** (`4a36c48`, xrpl-validator)
  rippled synthesizes the XRP bridge only in FlowCross (offer crossing). A payment's strands come from its own Paths plus the default path, so it reaches a book only where its path names one. cross_engine_to bridged unconditionally for IOU<->IOU, inventing a route the transaction never asked for.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): direct IOU payment requires an existing destination trust line** (`a6ef95a`, xrpl-validator)
  A Payment delivers an IOU by rippling through the issuer, which credits the destination's EXISTING trust line — rippled never opens a trust line for the receiver. Our apply_iou_direct phantom-created the destination's line and delivered, returning tesSUCCESS (with extra RippleState + owner-dir + dir-page mutations) where mainnet returned tecPATH_DRY (fee-only), because the destination did not trust the issuer.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): AMMDeposit owner-reserve guard for zero-LPToken depositors** (`385e129`, xrpl-validator)
  Same owner-reserve class as the TicketCreate/TrustSet fix: an AMMDeposit by an account holding ZERO LPTokens opens (or funds) an LPToken trust line and so must keep XRP above accountReserve(ownerCount + 1). The native engine skipped this and over-applied (tesSUCCESS, 4-5 muts) where mainnet returned tecINSUF_RESERVE_LINE (net_muts=1).
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): enforce owner reserve on object creation — TicketCreate + TrustSet** (`bcf7e5c`, xrpl-validator)
  The native engine minted owned objects without checking the account could afford the incremental owner reserve, returning tesSUCCESS where mainnet returned tecINSUFFICIENT_RESERVE (TicketCreate) / tecINSUF_RESERVE_LINE (TrustSet). Closes ~22 fresh-traffic scout divergences in one class fix.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): NFTokenCreateOffer token-existence preclaim + sandbox create-then-delete nets to nothing — 2 fresh-traffic finds** (`0a4d4f5`, xrpl-validator)
  Two shadow finds from the first hour of hourly scouting: NFTokenCreateOffer preclaim now walks the token owner's pages (sell→ Account, buy→sfOwner) and returns tecNO_ENTRY when the token is absent, per rippled NFTokenCreateOffer::preclaim findToken. Was accepting stale buy offers for burned/moved tokens (#105757083 1B7B0C8A). Probe preloads the owner's NFT pages for NFTokenCreateOffer.
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): per-leg AMM liquidity in bridged crossing — CORPUS 100% (1109/1109)** (`c333d93`, xrpl-validator)
  Each bridge leg is a BookStep of its own pair, so its book head competes with that pair's pool via multi-path FIB slices — mainnet filled A8830CA4's XAH leg from the XAH/XRP pool while its RLUSD leg crossed the book, and BE7884AA's missing partial fill was the same arbitration. Leg sources resolve independently (book offer or pool slice), the XRP slice flows through whichever pair wins, and pool legs advance their …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): multi-path FIB AMM offers in bridged crossing — corpus stable 1107/1109** (`f2d2bcb`, xrpl-validator)
  IOU↔IOU crossings are always two strands on mainnet, so the direct-pair pool competes as rippled's generateFibSeqOffer: CLOB-like slices sized 0.025% of the pool's starting balances scaled by the Fibonacci sequence, judged by the slice's AVERAGE quality (spot+slippage+fee). Stops the pool from beating books it loses to by razor margins (A8830CA4 no longer touches the XAH/RLUSD pool). No corpus regressions.
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): issuer account-hops, endpoint dedup, in-flight hygiene — all corpus payments match — corpus 99.8%** (`020c568`, xrpl-validator)
  Path account elements through the preceding hop's ISSUER are no-op re-anchors — accepted and skipped (true third-party rippling still unsupported). Endpoint currencies named as hops collapse via chain dedup. In-flight intermediates now leave NO trace: the sender's hop lines are restored byte-exact (Sandbox::forget for never-existed lines) and a mid-chain line-creation's directory droppings (both owners' roots + last …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): rippled QualityFunction limit-trim for AMM fills — corpus 99.5%** (`96dcd62`, xrpl-validator)
  Ported the exact single-path offer-crossing pipeline from rippled 3.2.0 (StrandFlow limitOut + BookStep post-check): when the AMM fill would beat the taker limit, the OUT request is trimmed via the pool QualityFunction (out = (1/q − b)/m under global Upward rounding, m/b at Near), re-swapped, and the ACHIEVED quality is re-checked with a 1e-7 relative tolerance granted only to trimmed requests. Directed roundings …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): me_muldiv 16-digit quotient scaling; AMM fee-adjusted spot; AMM turn in bridged crossing — corpus 99.3%** (`7d51d97`, xrpl-validator)
  me_muldiv truncated to ZERO when the denominator mantissa dwarfed the numerator (XRP drops x IOU 1e-11 mantissas) — the bridge slice sizing died on it; the quotient now keeps ~16 significant digits. AMM spot quality embeds the trading fee (rate = in / (out·(1−f))) and the direct-pair pool competes inside cross_bridged, anchored to the best book rate. DX_BRIDGE/DX_AMM env diagnostics stay in. 105663160 replays 65/65; …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): XRP autobridging for IOU↔IOU crossing; sender-reserve funding check — corpus 99.2%** (`7111b1f`, xrpl-validator)
  cross_bridged interleaves the direct book with the two-book XRP bridge (rippled FlowCross): peek both sources' head rates, consume one offer or bridge slice from the better one within the taker threshold; the XRP intermediate rides through the taker and nets out of the mutation set. live_head only condemns unfunded offers whose backing state is actually loaded (phantom deletions poisoned attempt 1) and never mutates …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): full AMM lifecycle parity — Create/Deposit/Withdraw on real legs — corpus 99.1%** (`a146364`, xrpl-validator)
  AMMCreate derives the pool account the rippled way (ripesha over sha512half(prefix0 ‖ parentHash ‖ ammKeylet), parent hash now threaded from the fixture header), creates the AMM AccountRoot + funds both legs via move_leg + mints creator LP tokens. Deposit/Withdraw move real legs between depositor and pool account and mint/burn the LP-token line (currency 0x03 ++ SHA512(minCur ‖ maxCur)[0..19], mainnet-verified). …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): Number-exact rounding, XLS-80 domain books, multi-hop payment strands, real reserve — corpus 98.8%** (`13197e6`, xrpl-validator)
  st_divide/st_multiply now round half-even folding the exact division remainder (the legacy +5/+7 truncation fudges double-rounded against the new nearest pass: 0EAE58BB fixed both ways, 42071037 proved the fudge had to go). DomainID (permissioned DEX) hashes into the book base for crossing AND placement — 105666804 replays 62/62. Payments execute multi-hop strands from the first path (chained book crossings, …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): real NFTokenPage model — mint/burn/accept/cancel/modify on structural page keys — corpus 98.2%** (`454d6c6`, xrpl-validator)
  New ledger::nftpage: page key = owner(20) ++ low-96 bound, max page anchor, chain walk via PreviousPageMin, sorted insert, empty-page delete + relink. NFTokenMint composes the real NFTokenID (flags‖fee‖issuer‖taxon^scramble‖seq off FirstNFTokenSequence+MintedNFTokens); AcceptOffer takes offer INDEXES (direct + brokered), moves the token between page sets, pays XRP with the id-embedded transfer fee, and clears offer …
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): real TicketCreate + OracleSet/OracleDelete transactors; tecDST_TAG_NEEDED — corpus 97.5%** (`d28de50`, xrpl-validator)
  TicketCreate: batch ticket creation off tx seq (rippled CreateTicket.cpp shape), owner-dir spill via shared dir_insert, TicketCount/OwnerCount and Sequence landing past the batch. OracleSet: XLS-47 upsert with pair-level series merge and 1/2-unit reserve; new oracle keylet (0x0052 'R', mainnet-verified). Payment preclaim gains the lsfRequireDestTag check. Closes all 4 TicketCreate + 2 OracleSet SKIP-STUBs and the …
- **feat(node): differential_probe RPC disk cache — immutable historical lookups, DX_CACHE override** (`a585ce8`, xrpl-validator)
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): faithful OfferCreate preclaim funding — tecUNFUNDED_OFFER — corpus 96.8%** (`5b1d386`, xrpl-validator)
  OfferCreate::preclaim returned the generic TxResult::Unfunded (tecUNFUNDED) and rejected whenever balance < the FULL sell amount. Both are wrong: rippled unfunds only when accountFunds(TakerGets) <= 0 (for XRP: balance minus reserve — partially funded offers still cross) and the code is tecUNFUNDED_OFFER. The old check was also XRP-only.
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): sell-side offer funding check — tecUNFUNDED_OFFER — corpus 96.8%** (`870eefd`, xrpl-validator)
  An OfferCreate can only sell what the account actually holds of the TakerGets asset. Our crossing engine checked the MAKER funding but never the taker: after the tfSell fix started crossing the full TakerGets, five arb offers from one account selling IOUs it holds NONE of (CLARITY, XALPHA) crossed and succeeded, where mainnet returns tecUNFUNDED_OFFER fee-only. Verified: the account CLARITY line was absent at seq-1.
- **feat(xrpl-ledger): tfSell crossing semantics — sell the whole TakerGets — corpus 96.3%** (`3a3f337`, xrpl-validator)
  A tfSell offer sells its ENTIRE TakerGets even if that acquires more than TakerPays; TakerPays is only a minimum. Our crossing engine capped each fill at rem_pays (the wanted amount) and terminated the walk once it was reached, so a tfSell that could fully sell only by taking the surplus was left with an unsold remainder — and a FillOrKill/ImmediateOrCancel sell was then wrongly killed. Mainnet tx 2042F8CE / …
- **fix(xrpl-ledger): trust-line lifecycle — receiver-only reserve, NoRipple, zero-balance deletion — corpus 95.6%** (`d547205`, xrpl-validator)
  Three faithful-port fixes in line_adjust, from rippled rippleCreditIOU / trustCreate: - trustCreate charges the reserve to the RECEIVER only. We bumped BOTH parties' OwnerCount, so the issuer accrued phantom reserves. The line still joins both owner directories; only one OwnerCount moves. - New lines get NoRipple on the receiver's side unless their account has DefaultRipple. - **fix(xrpl-ledger): route strand output to the destination — corpus 95.6%** (`bb1eefa`, xrpl-validator)

## XRPL research

### Automated research — candidate amendment ideas

_Ideas generated and prior-art-checked by the XRPL Amendment Board, an automated research tool (read-only board: [iceland.halcyon-names.io](https://iceland.halcyon-names.io/)). They are exploration leads, not proposals._

- **Fractional NFT Co-Ownership via Vault Shares** (nft)
  Add a tfNFTokenVault flag to VaultCreate (SingleAssetVault) letting the funding deposit be a single locked NFTokenID instead of a fungible amount; the
- **Scoped, Expiring Multisign Session Entries** (accounts)
  Add three optional SFields to each SignerEntry inside an ExpandedSignerList — sfTxTypeMask (bitmap of allowed tt codes), sfMaxAmount, and sfExpiration
- **Consensus-Enforced Reserve Backing for MPT Issuers** (audit)
  Add sfReserveAccount (AccountID) and sfMinReserveBps SFields to MPTokenIssuance, set at MPTokenIssuanceCreate and immutable unless the issuance carrie

| 08-25 12:55 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `42616e0` | fix(xrpl-ledger): a missing escrow is tecNO_TARGET, not tecNO_ENTRY — EscrowFinish and EscrowCancel, all four sites |
| 08-25 12:55 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `f28a6eb` | fix(xrpl-ledger): AMM pool admission is context-dependent — fib-slice average under multiPath, raw spot single-path, keyed on iteration-entry multiPath — two hundred eighty consecutive ledgers |
| 08-25 03:21 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `527956b` | fix(xrpl-ledger): AccountSet asf-to-lsf is a table, not a shift — two hundred fifty-eight consecutive ledgers |
| 08-25 01:21 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `af8bd2f` | fix(xrpl-ledger): the strand ub composes hop-0 trIn and the final DirectI's issuing rate — two hundred forty consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 23:15 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `390bff3` | fix(xrpl-ledger): the fwd chain carries the PRODUCED amount when the delta is exact, and a met-want hop flushes its excess input through the pool — two hundred thirty-nine consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 19:23 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `d94dc7f` | fix(xrpl-ledger): strand admission is judged once per iteration — an un-attempted self-removal resets the level anchor — TWO HUNDRED TWENTY CONSECUTIVE LEDGERS, THE WHOLE FETCHED RANGE |
| 08-24 18:21 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `cbfc29a` | fix(xrpl-ledger): the crossing walk's remainingOut is StrandFlow's fold, never a running subtraction — two hundred seventeen consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 17:18 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `7ec3a7c` | fix(xrpl-ledger): trustCreate NoRipple — creator side from the tx flags ONLY, peer side stamped when the PEER lacks DefaultRipple — two hundred eleven consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 16:15 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `5485685` | fix(xrpl-ledger): the underfunded crossing clamp threads the balance as the walk's GROSS cap — two hundred six consecutive ledgers |

| 08-24 11:41 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `8bef73f` | fix(xrpl-ledger): the delivered figure is rippled's actualOut — the sorted 16-digit fold, never an exact-width sum — one hundred ninety consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 10:35 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `c88dc80` | fix(xrpl-ledger): tearing down a redeemed LP line charges ONLY the holder — one hundred eighty-six consecutive ledgers |

| 08-24 03:25 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `737f067` | fix(xrpl-ledger): every spend-side net division is mulRatio-NEAREST — the live-line clamp was the fourth and last floor — one hundred sixteen consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 02:28 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `afb53c1` | fix(xrpl-ledger): a book strand's sin is GROSS — the round loop drained the NET pot with it — one hundred seven consecutive ledgers |
| 08-24 01:42 | 08-29 19:03 | xrpl-validator | `b14b09a` | fix(xrpl-ledger): direct and run-tail asks take the NET twin verbatim — dividing the gross pot cannot recover it — eighty-four consecutive ledgers |
